    await page.evaluate(payload=>{const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload)],{type:'application/json'}));const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='post-finishing-progress.json';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000)},{...verification,segment,samples,errors,...(typeof inventory==='undefined'?{}:{inventory}),...(typeof checks==='undefined'?{}:{checks})})
    await (await download).saveAs("/tmp/post-finishing-performance-final31-0.checkpoint.json")
  }
  const pdaSession={userId:'ID-F002_operator',loginId:'ID-F002_operator',userName:'后道验收操作员',roleId:'ROLE_OPERATOR',factoryId:'ID-F002',factoryName:'PT Prima Printing Center',loggedAt:'2026-10-10 10:00:00'}
  const create = async (device, route) => {
    const c = await page.context().browser().newContext({ viewport:device })
    await c.addInitScript(({session,route})=>{
      localStorage.setItem('fcs_pda_session',JSON.stringify(session))
      window.pfLastMutation=0;window.pfPendingHandlers=0;window.pfPendingTransactions=0;window.pfMeasurements=[];window.pfHandlerErrors=[];window.pfLongTasks=[];new PerformanceObserver(list=>window.pfLongTasks.push(...list.getEntries().map(e=>({start:e.startTime,duration:e.duration})))).observe({type:'longtask',buffered:true})
      const originalAdd=EventTarget.prototype.addEventListener
      // Await the actual async app handler, including module loading and save.
      // Browser timestamps end at the rendered result, before host-tool polling.
      EventTarget.prototype.addEventListener=function(type,listener,options){
        if(((this instanceof Element && this.id==='app') || this===document) && ['click','input','change','keydown','submit'].includes(type) && typeof listener==='function' && listener.constructor.name==='AsyncFunction'){
          const wrapped=function(event){
            const result=listener.call(this,event)
            if(result&&typeof result.then==='function'){
              window.pfPendingHandlers++
              Promise.resolve(result).finally(()=>{window.pfPendingHandlers--;window.pfLastMutation=performance.now()}).catch(error=>window.pfHandlerErrors.push(String(error)))
            }
            return result
          }
          return originalAdd.call(this,type,wrapped,options)
        }
        return originalAdd.call(this,type,listener,options)
      }
      const originalTransaction=IDBDatabase.prototype.transaction
      IDBDatabase.prototype.transaction=function(...args){
        const tx=originalTransaction.apply(this,args);window.pfPendingTransactions++
        const done=()=>{window.pfPendingTransactions--;window.pfLastMutation=performance.now()}
        tx.addEventListener('complete',done,{once:true});tx.addEventListener('abort',done,{once:true});return tx
      }
      new MutationObserver(()=>{window.pfLastMutation=performance.now()}).observe(document,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,characterData:true})
      const inView=e=>{const b=e.getBoundingClientRect();return b.top<innerHeight&&b.bottom>0&&b.width&&b.height&&getComputedStyle(e).visibility!=='hidden'}
      const targetReady=r=>r.previousMain&&document.querySelector('main')===r.previousMain?false:r.selector?Boolean(document.querySelector(r.selector)):r.title?[...document.querySelectorAll('h1,h2')].some(e=>e.textContent.trim()===r.title):[...document.querySelectorAll('body *')].some(e=>e.children.length===0&&e.textContent.trim()===r.text)
      let generation=0
      window.pfBegin=(kind,expected,start=performance.now())=>{
        const current=++generation;window.pfMeasurementGeneration=current
        const check=()=>{
          if(current!==generation)return
          const images=[...document.querySelectorAll('main img,[data-testid] img,[role="dialog"] img')].filter(inView)
          const qr=[...document.querySelectorAll('[data-real-qr]')].filter(inView)
          if(targetReady(expected)&&window.pfPendingHandlers===0&&window.pfPendingTransactions===0&&performance.now()-window.pfLastMutation>=50&&images.every(i=>i.complete)&&qr.every(e=>e.querySelector('svg'))){
            requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
              if(current!==generation)return
              if(window.pfPendingHandlers||window.pfPendingTransactions||performance.now()-window.pfLastMutation<50){requestAnimationFrame(check);return}
              window.pfMeasurements.push({generation:current,kind,ms:performance.now()-start})
            }))
          }else requestAnimationFrame(check)
        }
        requestAnimationFrame(check)
      }
      originalAdd.call(document,'click',event=>{if(!event.isTrusted)return;const image=event.target.closest('[data-post-finishing-action="full-flow-zoom-image"]');const changesPage=event.target.closest('[data-nav], [data-action="open-tab"][data-tab-href]')||event.target.closest('button[type="submit"],button:not([type])')?.closest('form');window.pfBegin(image?'image':'action',image?{selector:'[role="dialog"] img'}:changesPage?{selector:'main h1',previousMain:document.querySelector('main')}:route)},true)

    const p=await c.newPage();p.setDefaultTimeout(4000);p.on('dialog',d=>d.accept());p.on('pageerror',e=>errors.push(e.message))
    return {c,p}
  }
  const measured=async(p,kind)=>{
    await p.waitForFunction(kind=>window.pfMeasurements?.some(s=>s.kind===kind&&s.generation===window.pfMeasurementGeneration),kind)
    return p.evaluate(kind=>window.pfMeasurements.filter(s=>s.kind===kind).at(-1).ms,kind)
  }
  const diagnostics=async p=>p.evaluate(()=>({visibility:document.visibilityState,focus:document.hasFocus(),longtasks:window.pfLongTasks,resources:performance.getEntriesByType('resource').sort((a,b)=>b.duration-a.duration).slice(0,5).map(e=>({url:e.name,duration:e.duration,size:e.transferSize}))}))
  for(const r of selected){

    for(const device of devices)for(let trial=1;trial<=5;trial++){
      const {c,p}=await create(device,r)
      try{
        await p.goto(base+r.path,{waitUntil:'domcontentloaded'});const cold=await measured(p,'load')
        samples.push({route:r.path,device,trial,kind:'cold',ms:cold,pass:cold<=1000,diagnostics:await diagnostics(p)})
        await p.reload({waitUntil:'domcontentloaded'});const refresh=await measured(p,'load')
        samples.push({route:r.path,device,trial,kind:'refresh',ms:refresh,pass:refresh<=1000,diagnostics:await diagnostics(p)})
        await p.evaluate(({path,route})=>{window.pfBegin('list-navigation',{...route,previousMain:document.querySelector('main')});history.pushState({},'',path);window.dispatchEvent(new PopStateEvent('popstate'))},{path:r.path===prefix+'qc-orders'?prefix+'qc-workbench':prefix+'qc-orders',route:{title:r.path===prefix+'qc-orders'?'执行质检':'质检清单'}})

        const navigation=await measured(p,'navigation')
        samples.push({route:r.path,device,trial,kind:'navigation',ms:navigation,pass:navigation<=1000,diagnostics:await diagnostics(p)})
        if(r.path.includes('print?') && device===devices[0]) {
          const begin=await p.evaluate(()=>performance.now())
          await p.pdf({path:`/tmp/post-finishing-print-${segment}-${selected.indexOf(r)}-${trial}.pdf`,format:'A4',printBackground:true})
          const finish=await p.evaluate(()=>performance.now())
          samples.push({route:r.path,device,trial,kind:'pdf-generation',ms:finish-begin,pass:finish-begin<=1000})
        }
        if(trial===1&&device===devices[0]){
          inventory.push({route:r.path,controls:await p.evaluate(()=>[...document.querySelectorAll('main button,main a[data-nav],main input,main select,main textarea,[data-testid] button,[data-testid] input,[data-testid] select')].filter(e=>e.getBoundingClientRect().width&&e.getBoundingClientRect().height&&!e.disabled).map(e=>({tag:e.tagName,text:e.textContent.trim().slice(0,60),attrs:Object.fromEntries([...e.attributes].filter(a=>a.name.startsWith('data-')||a.name==='name').map(a=>[a.name,a.value]))})))})
          await p.screenshot({path:`/tmp/post-finishing-${segment}-${selected.indexOf(r)}-${device.width}.png`,fullPage:false})
        }
        // First interaction of each route and repeated trials includes default
        // quantity entry and large-image actions without changing data volume.
        const image=p.locator('main [data-post-finishing-action="full-flow-zoom-image"], [data-qc-spu-color-images] button').first()
        if(await image.count()){
          await image.click();
          const ms=await measured(p,'image');samples.push({route:r.path,device,trial,kind:'image',ms,pass:ms<=1000});await p.keyboard.press('Escape');const closeMs=await measured(p,'image-close-escape');samples.push({route:r.path,device,trial,kind:'image-close-escape',ms:closeMs,pass:closeMs<=1000})
        }
        const handlerErrors=await p.evaluate(()=>window.pfHandlerErrors);errors.push(...handlerErrors.map(error=>`${r.path} handler: ${error}`))
      }catch(e){errors.push(`${r.path} ${device.width} trial ${trial}: ${e.message}`)}finally{await c.close();await checkpoint()}
    }
  }
  return {segment,browser:page.context().browser().version(),service:base,data:'静态 3 个生产单 / 15 次回货 / 75 条 SKU',samples,inventory,errors,passed:errors.length===0&&samples.every(s=>s.pass)}
}
)(page);
